# Rewrite test report

Slice 1 implements Rust CLI/server sessions and one agy turn with the Svelte browser UI; Slice 3 adds permission policy, the hook, approvals and mid-turn steering; Slice 4 adds goals, the check command, the independent verifier and plan mode; Slice 5 adds background jobs, loops, daily schedules and keep-awake; Slice 6 adds git worktrees, change review and project files; Slice 7 adds the Terminal panel; Slice 8 adds attachments and dictation; Slice 9a completes the remaining engine routes; Slice 9b adds settings, preferences and usage charts; Slice 9c adds sidebar management, search and shared split view; Slice 9d adds composer commands, autocomplete, history, shortcuts and header controls; Slice 9e adds transcript rendering and safe permission retries; Slice 10 implements the native shell and proves the macOS installer, with native OS integration still Pending. Slice 11 proves storage compatibility and the persisted defaults; Slice 12 proves session lifecycle and turn edges; Slice 13 proves quota routing, walls and error channels; Slice 14 carries the owner's requests of 2026-10-09; Slice 15 builds and checks the Linux installers; Slices 16 and 16b prove the architecture and tooling requirements; Slice 17a proves the CLI commands. **903 of 1162 acceptance items are Passed; 259 remain Pending.** Slice 9f adds layout and interaction edges and folds 33 fully proved Slice 2 account rows; 32 partial account rows remain Pending. [Account proof](../../tests/rewrite/slice9f-account-coverage.json) replaces the earlier proposal as the acceptance map. The inventory contains 1009 baseline items and 149 explicit rewrite requirements and owner requests in [FEATURES.md](FEATURES.md). Source review, legacy tests and Python captures are references; the rows below name actual rewrite checks. A Pending row may have passing subset tests, but its remaining acceptance is explicit.

The fresh independent macOS arm64 gate on 2026-10-07 ran against 440 unchanged source hashes. `main` remained clean at `17ec296`; no push or cutover occurred. Synthetic processes were stopped after validation. [Final verification](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-independent-final-summary.json) records commands, hashes, counts and cleanup.

| Command or review | Result | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `just check` | Passed: generated-type freshness, rustfmt, all-target clippy with warnings denied, cargo-deny, strict Svelte/TypeScript, ESLint and Prettier | [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-independent-final-check.log) |
| `just test` | Passed: 51 Rust workspace tests, 28 Vitest tests in 6 files, and 6 Chromium scenarios; zero failures, ignored tests or flaky browser results | [Test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-independent-final-test.log) |
| `just build` | Passed for Rust engine and browser UI. Sidecar desktop bundle remains Pending until Slice 10 | [Build log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-independent-final-build.log) |
| Separate owned exporter regression | 1 passing example test, not included in the 51-test fresh workspace count; detects changed, missing and extra nested generated files | [Owned affected-crate test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-fixes-tests.log) |
| Independent source/architecture checks | GREEN for current Slice 1 sources; no runtime parity claim for future features | [Pattern review](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-pattern-review.json) |
| Independent failure-path review | GREEN after repairs: early conversation-ID durability, descendant cancellation/inherited pipes, deletion admission | [Quality review](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-quality-review.log), [runtime reproduction](../../.handoff/evidence/slice1-quality-fixed-reproduction.json) |

Runtime contract replay covers exactly **22 of 172 captured cases**, including one SSE case with eight original first-turn samples, across the ten currently implemented routes. The remaining 150 cases and 65 routes stay Pending. The separate [core round-trip test](../../crates/core/src/event_tests.rs) preserves every field in all 160 captured samples and 14 event kinds; schema round-trip proof does not imply runtime behavior for all those events. [Replay scope](../../tests/rewrite/slice1-scope.json) and [comparison rules](../../tests/rewrite/README.md) describe the tested subset.

All application handoff, quota routing, approvals, goals/verifier, jobs, loop/schedule, worktrees, terminal, dictation and remaining UI parity retain their individual Pending rows. Native shell, zoom, window persistence, installation, Linux/Windows execution, full CI, real-account smoke and live cost records remain Pending. This validation used synthetic data under `~/Desktop/test`, with no paid agy calls or real-account changes. Existing local backup and continuity evidence do not establish final runtime migration compatibility.

## Slice 3: permissions, hook, approvals and steering

On 2026-10-07 on macOS arm64, `just check` passed (generated types, rustfmt, clippy with warnings denied, cargo-deny, strict Svelte/TypeScript, ESLint, Prettier) and `just test` passed 143 Rust tests, 59 Vitest tests and 30 Chromium scenarios with no failures. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice3-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice3-test.log).

Eleven more captured cases now replay exactly: permissions get/put/error, send-long, send-queued, session-steer and the eleven approval request/wait/answer/read cases. Real hook round trips run fake agy, which invokes the built `antiquota hook` for a tool call that waits for an HTTP or browser answer. Remaining Pending rows in this area: F-133 (stack-import and file-edit entry points arrive with the settings slice), F-145 (the 60-second poll cap is enforced but not exercised), F-464 (header state labels), F-513 (steered message label), F-581 (expired-request message), F-582 (approval notification) and F-1030 (steering lives in the approvals service, not yet a feature module).

## Slice 4: goals, verifier and plan mode

On 2026-10-07 on macOS arm64, `just check` passed and `just test` passed 169 Rust tests, 62 Vitest tests and 33 Chromium scenarios. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice4-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice4-test.log).

Goal runs use the real engine, fake agy and the platform shell for checks; the verifier is a separate fresh agy call. Captured goal-empty, goal-resume-empty, goal-resume and goal-delete cases replay exactly; goal-create and goal-get-met depend on earlier job state and are compared field by field instead. Remaining Pending rows here: F-203 (a job's completion wake arrives with the jobs slice), F-204 (retry waits are proven in the feature module; app-level cancellation of the wait is not exercised), F-212 (a quota error during the verifier call itself), F-163 (the fresh-conversation jobs instruction), F-179 (the allowance reset per user message), F-305 (Ctrl-C in the goal CLI) and F-564 (Execute with approvals in the browser).

On 2026-10-07 on macOS arm64, `just check` passed and `just test` passed 189 Rust tests, 65 Vitest tests and 34 Chromium scenarios. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice5-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice5-test.log).

## Slice 7: terminal

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, `just check` passed and `just test` passed 212 Rust tests, 70 Vitest tests and 38 Chromium scenarios. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice7-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice7-test.log).

Captured terminal create, input, resize, restart, kill and missing cases replay exactly when the login shell is zsh (the captured shell name); with another shell the same requests are checked without the shell-name field. The stream is read for real and the typed command's output is asserted, as the capture's comparison rules require. The captured `/vendor/xterm*` static files belonged to the legacy page; the rewrite bundles xterm into its own lazily loaded chunk (intentional difference). Remaining Pending rows here: F-276 (Windows ConPTY with COMSPEC needs a Windows run), F-284 (burst coalescing is implemented but not measured), F-309, F-620, F-623, F-625, F-630, F-631, F-636 to F-638 (not individually asserted), and the dictation rows F-285 to F-289.

## Slice 8: attachments and dictation

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, `just check` passed and `just test` passed 224 Rust tests, 87 Vitest tests and 45 Chromium scenarios. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice8-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice8-test.log), [screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice8/).

The captured upload, download, limit, header-only and missing attachment cases and the transcribe, no-speech, empty and limit cases replay exactly; the captured 404 for a spaced name is replaced by the B-021 correction. Chromium's microphone is a recorded speech WAV (made with the macOS `say` command) or a silent WAV, so recording, the silence guard and insertion run in a real browser against the fake agent. Live agy checks transcribed WebM Opus, Opus-in-MP4 and AAC M4A recordings correctly. They also showed that the legacy call's skipped permissions let agy run commands and read other conversations; the rewrite no longer skips them (W-010). A terminal Playwright run exposed out-of-order keystrokes, now fixed (W-009). One full-gate run hit a connection reset in `abrupt_engine_exit_after_init_resumes_the_durable_conversation` while live agy calls loaded the machine; five isolated reruns and the final gate passed, and the cause is not identified ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice8-test-flake-1.log)). Remaining Pending rows in this area: F-159 (an attachment-only title fallback), F-160, F-162, F-165 and F-355 (structured message kinds, job folder access and permission flags).

## Slice 9a: recovery, stack and remaining routes

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, fresh `just check` passed and `just test` passed 245 Rust tests, 88 Vitest tests and 46 Chromium scenarios, with no failed, ignored or flaky final-gate tests. The known abrupt-engine connection-reset test passed. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9a-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9a-test.log), [screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice9a/), [49-row coverage mapping](../../tests/rewrite/slice9a-coverage.json).

All remaining route methods are implemented. Session, settings, diagnostics, folder and usage captures replay through HTTP; usage dates follow only the fixture's explicit local-calendar rule. The `stack-reseed` capture is retained unchanged and replaced by an assertion of the protected-hook/history correction. Pure and in-memory tests bound handoff rendering, isolate fork ownership and enforce newest-message search. Additional HTTP and adapter tests cover stack imports, editor limits, metadata without sign-in, merged shell output and cancellation, concurrent notes, empty-file rewind, later user edits and ambiguous old checkpoints. Chromium proves compact/clear retain visible history and a fork restores after reload, using the existing event connection. UI command and settings entry points belong to 9b–9d.

Initial focused runs failed on a two-account capture setup, incorrect test event fields, note-prefix expectation and MCP fixture filename; those harness errors were corrected. Clippy initially rejected a complex tuple type, now replaced with a named work-turn struct. Early console-only failures were not saved as separate files. The first browser scenario requested clear before turn-end and received the correct busy refusal; it now waits for idle ([initial browser log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9a-ui-initial-failure.log)). Review also found permission-path aliases, malformed-settings replacement, concurrent-note loss, shell lifecycle gaps and unsafe ambiguous snapshots; focused regressions pass after repairs. A final source inspection corrected unsigned stack metadata probes and both gates were rerun after that change.

This part adds 49 Passed rows, for 331 of 1144. Partial acceptance stays Pending: F-068 (routing n), F-091 (successful everything-copy transport), F-099 (exhaustive linked-parent editor proof), F-240 (explicit first-400 discovery stop), F-257 (closed stdin and configured 600-second limit), and F-356 (default/invalid turn transport). Slice 2 evidence remains scheduled for 9f. Native shell, WebKit AAC dictation, live authorization, Linux/Windows execution and CI are not checked here. Live provider calls, tokens and cost: zero. No real account configuration, legacy checkout or cutover changed.

## Slice 9b: settings, preferences, accounts and usage

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, final `just check` passed and `just test` passed 245 Rust tests, 108 Vitest tests and 51 Chromium scenarios, with zero failed, ignored, skipped or flaky final results. The abrupt-engine connection-reset test passed. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-test.log), [screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice9b/), [107-row coverage mapping](../../tests/rewrite/slice9b-coverage.json).

Thirteen settings sections share the application-owned settings cache. Defaults and numeric endpoints persist through real HTTP; generations preserve dirty inputs and newer editors through slow reads, saves and deletion. Private stack editors create validated templates, compare CLI and IDE resources, import differing entries and retain every source byte. Duplicate creation refuses existing private items, including the owned permission hook. Chart.js is used directly; ledger statistics, period selection, cached error labels, accent pixels, empty days and bounded keyboard/hover tooltips pass in dark and light themes. Presentation and independent browser notification choices use compatible device storage. Existing account cards, rotation, routing history and synthetic sign-in tests pass again; their UI evidence is folded here, while account/backend policy correlation remains in 9f. Captured settings, stack and usage HTTP fixtures run in the same gate. Successful CLI everything-copy now also proves protected-hook and conversation-history preservation.

Initial focused runs found TypeScript narrowing, keyed-each and fixture-shape errors, followed by browser selector, compact-navigation and incomplete-model-fixture errors; each was corrected. [Initial focused check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-focused-check.log), [initial browser log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-ui-initial.log), [second](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-ui-second.log), [third](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-ui-third.log), [fourth](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-ui-fourth.log). The first whole test gate failed an old Accounts shortcut expectation and a trace-file collision caused by overlapping browser runs; browser gates now run serially ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-test-initial-failure.log)). Review found duplicate creation overwriting private files, stale default reads, late saves/deletes closing newer editors, and cached usage labelled with a failed requested period. Focused regressions pass after fixes. A later whole gate failed the startup-metadata helper in one account test ([failure](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-test-metadata-failure.log)); the 43-test diagnostic account rerun passed ([diagnostics](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-metadata-diagnostic.log)). The readiness helper queried account overviews every ten milliseconds before model startup. Overview checks briefly acquire the real account lease; that can make the one startup model acquisition skip and leave the helper waiting until the normal long poll interval. The helper now waits for a new fake model-call record before reading account busy states. It retains detailed failure diagnostics; production lease and cached-model behavior are unchanged. An independent synthetic lock-interposition probe reproduces the same mechanism against the compiled adapter and service ([proof](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9b-account-overview-race/README.md)). The original failure did not retain detailed diagnostics, so its exact scheduling is unproven. The probe initially recursed and aborted; the corrected probe passed twice.

This part adds 107 Passed rows, for 438 of 1144. Combined General, Appearance and Engine rows remain Pending until native adapters and their full acceptance pass: F-669, F-670 and F-681. Navigation attention/path assertions, all diagnostic fields, first-message permission integration and positive restart UI proof remain Pending (F-684, F-772, F-704, F-775). Native autostart, zoom, notification permission, reveal, external sign-in, WebKit AAC recording and real-account authorization belong to Slice 10. Slice 2 backend evidence and the remaining UI edges belong to 9f. Linux/Windows execution and CI are not checked here. Live provider calls, tokens and cost: zero. No real account configuration, legacy checkout or cutover changed.

## Slice 9c: sidebar, search and shared split view

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, final `just check` and `just test` pass: 245 Rust tests, 129 Vitest tests and 61 Chromium scenarios, with zero final failed, ignored, skipped or flaky results. The known abrupt-engine connection-reset case passes. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-test.log), [screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice9c/), [55-row coverage mapping](../../tests/rewrite/slice9c-coverage.json).

Sidebar rows show relative time, status, worktrees and goals. Pinned groups, project recency, archived/current visibility, device collapse and temporary search expansion are proven. Session and project menus have bounded placement, keyboard wrapping, focus restoration and outside dismissal. Rename, pin/unpin, fork, archive/unarchive, project creation defaults, copy path and canceled/confirmed individual and bulk deletion run against the isolated engine; deletion retains project files. Debounced content search rejects stale results and exposes title, author, snippet, time and folder.

Split uses two focused session components on one event connection. Each selection has its own transcript, while session-keyed drafts, attachments, scroll and caret follow it across swaps. Restored IDs are validated, split hides the drawer, right controls omit sidebar/drawer/nested split, and both columns remain below the sidebar breakpoint. Opening, swapping, closing, reload, shared events, streaming search focus and cross-origin legacy control-message rejection pass. Dictation cancellation rejects late recognition, microphone and transcription work after a selection change or pane disposal; late streams release their tracks. Submitted transcription requests may finish, but canceled results are discarded. All existing Rust domain/application and captured HTTP replay tests pass; fixtures are unchanged.

The first focused check found eleven exact-optional, store and nullable-value TypeScript errors ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-focused-check-initial.log)); corrected check reports zero errors and warnings. The initial browser run failed four ambiguous/mismatched test selectors and search focus during streams ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-ui-initial.log)); all six scenarios passed after corrections ([second](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-ui-second.log)). Expanded proof then found scroll restoration losing the old position and an empty-page hash navigation test that did not reload ([third](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-ui-third.log)); save-before-render and explicit reload fixed both ([fourth](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-ui-fourth.log)). The ten-scenario final focused browser run passed ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-ui-final.log)). Independent synthetic review reproduced stale rename targets, delayed preferred refresh overriding selection, overlapping swaps duplicating sessions, streaming focus churn, duplicate-title menu keys and late dictation changing another draft. Regressions cover the fixes. Browser checks also verify empty-menu Escape, action focus and cancellation on swap/close. The first whole gate passed Rust and Vitest but failed one scroll scenario (60/61 Chromium, [log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-test-initial-failure.log)). Draft and snapshot updates retriggered the scroll effect before the browser delivered its scroll event. Stable derived inputs and measuring the viewport before rendering preserve manual scroll even under that timing. The affected scenario passed three focused repetitions ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9c-scroll-regression.log)); the final whole gate passes without retries. A final source check also corrected collapse-other to include pinned-only and hidden archived parent folders; its focused regression passes.

This part adds 55 Passed rows, for 493 of 1144. Native project reveal remains Pending (F-443); prompt history belongs to 9d (F-453). Combined all-menu/all-shortcut rows remain Pending until native and composer entry points pass (F-1105, F-1112). Native zoom, WebKit AAC recording, lifecycle, menus, tray and real-account authorization remain in Slice 10. Linux/Windows execution and native desktop checks have not run in this part. Live provider calls, tokens and cost: zero. No real account configuration, legacy checkout or cutover changed.

## Slice 9d: composer, commands and header

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, final `just check` passes all checks, including advisories and zero Svelte errors or warnings. Final `just test` passes 247 Rust tests, 244 Vitest tests in 33 files and 79 Chromium scenarios, with zero final failed, ignored, skipped or flaky results. The known abrupt-engine connection-reset case passes. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-test.log), [89-row coverage mapping](../../tests/rewrite/slice9d-coverage.json), [sixteen inspected background browser screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice9d/).

All 31 slash commands dispatch through the application-owned composer controller. Unknown or invalid commands remain local; successful actions clear only their accepted draft. Shell output records the project working folder and reaches the next prompt. Notes append to existing GEMINI.md and report its path. Rewind requires a positive turn and confirmation, preserves canceled checkpoints and refuses later user edits. Export fetches durable history and includes every supported role, reasoning, tools, turns and notices with a sanitized sixty-character filename. Typed job and loop requests come from Rust; captured HTTP compatibility remains unchanged. Every existing Rust application and HTTP replay test passes.

Autocomplete shares the command reference, inserts file mentions at the caret, ignores stale queries and closes after a tested 100 ms blur delay. Prompt history contains explicit durable prompts and accepted local input, preserves the saved draft, retains repeated occurrences without duplicating echoed events, and isolates sessions. Model families preserve effort, retain unknown IDs and expose quota pools; verifier options include concrete IDs and effort labels. Both permission entry points confirm bypass with an accurate warning that antiquota rules are ignored. Header identity, worktree origin, every activity state, cumulative usage, page title, inline rename and context/account meters pass. Shortcuts target the active pane; creation sends its initial draft to the actual new session, and deletion selects the next recent session or the start screen.

Independent synthetic review reproduced deleted-selection fallback, draft overwrite during creation, a late goal save closing a newer dialog, busy inline rename discarding edits, deferred upload and dictation focus, stale history after reload, an undefined shell color token and an inaccurate bypass warning. Focused unit and four browser race regressions cover the repairs. The initial strict check found four warnings; a later check found one fixture type error and one unused-style warning. Both were corrected ([initial](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-check-initial.log), [later](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-check-third.log)). The first expanded browser run passed seven of fourteen scenarios; seven failures came from argv, background prompt, quota-cache, loading, timer and selector assumptions ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-ui-initial.log)). The second run passed thirteen; missing-turn rewind validation had not been applied by the scripted edit ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-ui-second.log)). Corrected source and assertions pass all fourteen ([third](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-ui-third.log)). Additional cwd, append and last-session deletion proof first failed an abbreviated saved-path expectation, then passed with the actual full path ([first](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-final-edge-proof.log), [second](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-final-edge-proof-second.log)).

The first whole gate failed one outdated cancellation unit case, which supplied `/rewind` without its now-required turn ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-test-initial-failure.log)); the corrected `/rewind 1` case passes. A later whole gate failed a one-shot fixture lease acquisition after an idle HTTP snapshot ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-test-lease-failure.log)). Account overview briefly acquires the actual lease, so the snapshot cannot guarantee a later acquisition. Fixture setup now retries the atomic acquisition with an eight-second bound; Python/Rust exclusion assertions remain nonblocking. A real held-lock regression, 44 account tests and a separate cross-language lock test pass ([account regression](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-account-lease-regression.log), [cross-language regression](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-cross-lock-regression.log)). Independent source review confirms the mechanism; the exact original thread scheduling was not retained. Production account and model behavior is unchanged. The next whole gate passed Rust and Vitest plus 78 of 79 browser scenarios but found an ambiguous old approval-text selector after the header gained the same label ([log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-test-approval-selector-failure.log)). A header-scoped selector passes both approval scenarios ([regression](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9d-approval-regression.log)), and the final whole gate passes without retries.

This part adds 89 Passed rows, for 582 of 1144. Typed transcript notices, including historical external-edit rewind annotations, remain in 9e (F-550). Native save, zoom, menus, tray, lifecycle, WebKit AAC recording and real-account authorization remain in Slice 10. Combined native shortcut/menu rows stay Pending. Linux/Windows execution and native desktop checks have not run here. Live provider calls, tokens and cost: zero. No real account configuration, legacy checkout or cutover changed.

## Slice 9e: transcript rendering and safe permission retries

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, final `just check` passed and `just test` passed 262 Rust tests, 369 Vitest tests in 37 files and 88 Chromium scenarios, with zero failed, ignored, skipped or flaky final-gate tests. The known abrupt-engine connection-reset test passed. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-test.log), [12 inspected screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice9e/), [49-row coverage mapping](../../tests/rewrite/slice9e-coverage.json).

Escaped Markdown renders the legacy grammar, opens HTTP links and read-only file links, and copies exact code or completed replies. Stable chronological items retain tool expansion through real SSE completion. Tool helpers show readable actions, bounded previews, complete bodies and grouped counts; reasoning remains available in both device detail modes. Browser tests cover prompt origins, goal tooltips, steering, turns, typed notices, command and edit retries, historical outside-edit annotations and the exact scroll boundary. Both detail states survive reload. Rewind preserves later user bytes through the existing B-022 refusal.

Current and historical denial suggestions come from core policy. Historical normalization leaves durable bytes unchanged. Permanent-rule validation now recognizes executable paths, quotes, escapes, versions and bracket/brace expansions; risky flags still require a decision. The additive permission PUT appends atomically under the adapter changes lock and preserves deny rules and unknown settings. Concurrent grants, invalid batches, corrupt settings, stored history and current write-denial events have app, adapter and HTTP regressions. Existing permission captures and goal captures matched unchanged. All file-opening callers share request ownership so an old drawer, Markdown or composer response cannot replace newer viewer content.

Initial focused checks caught a scripted string-escaping parse error, an unsafe find suggestion and two browser-test type errors. The initial 15-scenario browser run passed six and failed nine: old spinner/expansion assertions, popup interception limited to the first page, tooltip/class/whitespace assumptions, a premature permission read and a global live-class selector. Its partial-reply assertion also assumed incomplete provider output was durable; the current provider protocol stores replies when their DONE step arrives, so failed-turn proof checks the error, end status and removed transcript markers. The second run passed thirteen and failed two on a multi-notice locator and an incorrect expectation that GET permissions also returns deny; the tests now read the documented settings response. The eight new transcript scenarios then passed, and the added cross-surface viewer race passed separately. [Initial browser log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-ui-initial.log), [second browser log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-ui-second.log), [third browser log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-ui-third.log), [viewer race](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-viewer-browser.log).

The first full check failed on six lint errors; bounded generated HTML has a scoped lint allowance, and regex/spacing lint errors were corrected. A subsequent lint run still found a quoted literal after formatting, then passed after its correction. The first full test passed Rust and Vitest but failed two of 88 Chromium scenarios because the old Goal selector also matched the new goal notice; exact selectors corrected it. Final full gates passed after these changes. One focused adapter assertion incorrectly expected an explicit risky-flag rule to be rejected at storage; it was corrected because compatible loaded matching already forces that command to ask, while suggestions refuse it. These failures and traces are retained. [First check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-check-initial.log), [first full test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-test-initial.log), [retained browser failures](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice9e-gate-failures/), [focused permission failure](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9e-permissions-test-initial.log). Node emitted its localStorage experimental warning; Playwright emitted its color-environment warning. Neither is a failed test.

This part adds 49 Passed rows, for 631 of 1144. Slice 9f covers the remaining layout/interaction edges and account evidence. Native save, zoom, menus, tray, lifecycle, WebKit AAC recording and real-account authorization remain in Slice 10. Linux/Windows execution and native desktop checks have not run here. Live provider calls, tokens and cost: zero. No real account configuration, legacy checkout or cutover changed.

## Slice 9f: layout, interaction edges and account acceptance

## Slice 10: native shell implementation and macOS package

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, final `just check` passes all checks, including zero Svelte errors/warnings and cargo-deny with the documented scoped GTK3 advisory exception. Final `just test` passes **292 Rust tests, 448 Vitest tests in 45 files and 117 Chromium scenarios**, with zero final failed, ignored, skipped or flaky results. The known `abrupt_engine_exit_after_init_resumes_the_durable_conversation` test passes. All existing captured HTTP replay tests pass; this slice introduces no HTTP route or fixture changes. Fresh `just build` succeeds. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-test.log), [build log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-build.log), [installed package proof](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-package-proof.json), [17-row coverage map](../../tests/rewrite/slice10-coverage.json).

The Tauri crate now belongs to the Rust workspace and bundles the single Rust engine plus the same browser UI. Discovery checks loopback origin, health identity/PID and nonempty token; redirects and foreign authenticated requests are rejected. App-owned launch appends a private log and detaches its process group. Retry, restart, unknown-work quit confirmation, typed application-menu/tray actions, scoped official native APIs, native webview zoom, traffic-light space, saved window geometry and offscreen recovery are implemented. Same-origin tray rediscovery preserves the current draft and event connection. Async native link, path and settings failures retain their initiating session/component. Recorder selection prefers AAC MP4 in native macOS WebKit; this is a mocked environment proof, not an actual recording. [ADR 0022](../adr/0022-native-shell-and-rust-sidecar.md) records the design and limits.

The macOS app and compressed UDZO DMG contain the Rust executable and UI, and the Applications shortcut resolves correctly. A read-only background mount and copied installation under Desktop/test pass deep strict ad hoc signature verification; bundled CLI help commands pass. No Developer ID signature, notarization or Gatekeeper approval is claimed. Linux/Windows workflow and installer code are committed, but those platforms and remote CI outcomes remain Pending until actual runs finish.

Eight screenshots were inspected: six Chromium screenshots use mocked Tauri APIs; `native-background-splash.png` is an actual background native starting splash. `native-initial-blank.png` is invalid rewrite proof: a bare computer-use relaunch accidentally attached to the existing legacy Python engine and captured its empty UI. It is retained as failed evidence and closes no row. Earlier direct launch briefly activated the owned test window; that launch was stopped. The final LaunchServices launch preserves the foreground application throughout its recorded trace and never moves existing user windows. [Screenshots](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice10/), [background foreground trace](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-background-launch-proof.json), [process sample](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-background-native-sample.txt).

The native test app reaches its splash and invokes startup, but stalls in the kernel filesystem open for its regular sandbox `state/engine.json` under Desktop/test before any HTTP request. OS access mediation is the leading hypothesis; its exact cause is unconfirmed. Narrow loopback ATS configuration and removing remote fonts from an ignored probe build did not resolve the stall and are not asserted as the cause. R-009 records that synchronous file opens can exceed the polling deadline. No permission dialog or Full Disk Access grant was accepted or bypassed. The user question about granting Desktop-folder access remains unanswered. Native B-001/B-002/B-003 proof, every actual application-menu/tray action, native permissions, window restart/persistence, split zoom at all four sizes and real WKWebView AAC MP4/ffprobe recording remain Pending. The owned test shell and synthetic sidecar were stopped; the existing legacy engine was left running. [Cleanup](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-native-cleanup.json).

Initial focused failures are retained: macOS mock sockets inherited nonblocking mode and triggered panic/SIGABRT; blocking accepted sockets and unwind-safe fixture cleanup fixed it. Native bridge tests initially passed 21/23 and found teardown/download regressions; lifetime guards and download-attribute handling repaired them. Initial native browser proof passed 2/3 and exposed incorrect focus/endpoint assumptions; final four scenarios pass. Scoped clippy first found four nested-condition/boolean warnings; the first full check then found test-module placement and the intentionally detached-child fixture lint. Corrections pass the final gate. Initial UI dependency/build/check/lint and scoped license failures are retained in their named slice10 logs. All final tests pass. Cargo-deny ignores only RUSTSEC-2024-0370 for the unmaintained proc-macro-error GTK3 build dependency; it has no available patched version. This remains R-010, not an undisclosed green security claim. [Initial engine failure](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-engine-focused-initial-failure.log), [bridge failure](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-native-focused-initial-failure.log), [native browser first run](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-native-browser-focused-first.log), [first full check failure](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-check-first-failure.log), [license/advisory investigation](../../.handoff/evidence/slice10-native-licenses-initial.log).

No paid provider call, token consumption or live-account change occurred: live cost is zero and the requested live budget is still unanswered. ## Slice 11: storage location and persisted defaults

On 2026-10-08 on macOS arm64, `just check` passed and `just test` passed 301 Rust tests and 448 Vitest tests; 116 of 117 Chromium scenarios passed. The failure was `goals.spec.ts` "pauses and resumes a goal": after Resume the goal stayed at round 0 within the 15-second limit while the machine was also running the local memory model. The same test then passed 12 of 12 times alone and passes in macOS CI, so it is recorded as a load-sensitive flake, not a fix. The untitled-session change (F-159) landed after that gate; its HTTP test, the media contract target, the application tests and Clippy pass. [Check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice11-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice11-test.log).

The rewrite now keeps state where the legacy app did. On POSIX the home comes from the account database rather than `HOME`, and a real `~/.agyloop` folder moves once to `~/.antiquota` with its links, own-stack paths and conversation store link (W-021). `settings.json` is read key by key, so one hand-edited number no longer resets every setting (W-020). A captured replay exposed that `serde_json::from_value` cannot rebuild flattened arbitrary-precision numbers; the reader reparses text instead, and a unit test keeps a 30-digit number. Every persisted default and its validation is proved through HTTP, including normalization, unknown keys, refused updates, the home-folder fallback and session modes. Untitled sessions take the first non-blank line or "(attachment)", as before (F-159).

This slice also repairs the cross-platform CI that failed after Slice 10. On Linux, glib made `u32::MAX.into()` ambiguous. On Windows, checkouts converted generated files to CRLF; `.gitattributes` now keeps LF. A macOS settings test raced the engine's one-time model save. Newer Clippy flagged a collapsible match on Linux and Windows only. Remaining Pending rows here: F-001 (Windows home branch needs a Windows run), F-003, F-006 to F-010, F-013, F-014 (other caches) and F-082 (three numeric fallbacks lack direct tests). See [ADR 0023](../adr/0023-storage-location-and-defaults.md).

On 2026-10-09 on Linux x86_64 (a cloud container running as root), `just check` passed and `just test` passed 336 Rust tests and 452 Vitest tests in 45 files. ## Slice 15: Linux installers and CI repairs

On 2026-10-09 on Linux x86_64, `sh app/scripts/build-app.sh` built the deb, rpm and AppImage, and the new `app/scripts/verify-linux-packages.sh` passed for all three ([package check](../../.handoff/evidence/linux-packages.log)). `just check` passed and `just test` passed 348 Rust tests, 458 Vitest tests in 45 files and 121 Chromium scenarios, with no failed, ignored or retried tests ([check log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice15-check.log), [test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice15-test.log), [coverage map](../../tests/rewrite/slice15-coverage.json)). F-971, F-976 and F-1060 are Passed.

The check found W-032: the packaged engine, started by itself, looked for its UI in the source tree path compiled into it, so `antiquota serve` from an installer served no UI. It now uses the `ui` folder beside its own `engine` folder. The deb also gains a long description.

The first gate run for this part failed once in `kill_ends_the_shell_and_its_children` ([failed run](../../.handoff/evidence/slice15-terminal-flake-test.log)). It led to W-033: killing a terminal waited for the end of its output, which a job that outlives the hangup keeps open. A disowned job reproduces it every time. The terminal now also ends when kill has reaped the shell, and `kill_ends_the_terminal_when_a_job_outlives_the_shell` covers it.

The commit before this part fixed CI, which had failed on every push to `rust`: Windows Clippy rejected a test variable that only Unix changes, and the Linux runner ran out of disk while building installers after the test build. CI now also installs `cpio` and `rpm2cpio` and runs the package check after the Linux installer build. The next CI run passed on macOS and Linux, including the Linux installers and their check. On Windows it reached the tests for the first time and found W-035, a failed removal of plain files on Windows. A local gate run also found W-034, a broken pipe in fake-agy's hook handoff. Both are fixed, and `just test` now runs every Rust test target before it fails, so one run lists every failure. The following Windows run listed about 40 failures in 13 targets. Most were tests written for POSIX: paths joined with `/`, `sh` command lines and LF output. Three were product defects on Windows: W-037 (ConPTY waited for a cursor answer, so sign-in and shells hung), W-038 (`\\?\` paths kept by the store) and W-039 (copied hooks kept the source path). Tests now use the platform shell's syntax and line ending, and replay compares Windows path forms as equal. The CI rows (F-973 to F-977 and F-1139 to F-1144) stay Pending until a run passes on all three systems.

Three reader regressions prove complete length/chunked frames survive a subsequent reset, truncated frames fail, and unframed responses still require a clean EOF. Final `just test` passed 445 Rust tests, 458 Vitest tests in 45 files and all 122 Chromium scenarios in one run. No final failures, ignored tests, skipped scenarios or flaky cases. [Final test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice17b-test.log), [browser results](../../.handoff/evidence/slice17b-e2e.json). After the approved live goal proof below, the count is 862 Passed and 288 Pending. The review also reproduced stale CLI reconnect completion, duplicate reconnect replies and split Windows cursor queries; Windows replay normalizes non-path strings as paths. Those six review defects remain unfixed. Actual native Mac zoom, window lifecycle, menus, tray, permissions and WebKit AAC proof remain Pending. The owner subsequently approved live tests, and the prepared one-round goal and verifier passed within the two-minute limit. Latest GitHub jobs did not start because billing minutes were unavailable. This part uses `[skip ci]` on its authorized `rust` push; no workflow was dispatched and no CI success is claimed.

The approved real goal used `gemini-3.8-flash-high` for both calls and completed in 56.852 seconds. The app's hook requested approval for only `smoke.txt`; Allow once admitted that write. The turn's recorded tools wrote and read that file, whose exact bytes are `READY` plus one newline. The configured check exited 0 and the fresh read-only verifier returned done. Two agy invocations produced five completed generation steps. The watchdog did not need to stop them. Its first setup mistakenly looked for a token in engine discovery JSON; reading the existing GUI-token file corrected that before any call started. [Live usage proof](../../.handoff/evidence/slice17b-live-usage-proof.json), [watchdog](../../.handoff/evidence/slice17b-live-watch.json), [completed goal](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice17b/live-goal-met.jpg), [scoped write approval](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice17b/live-write-approval.jpg).

## Slice 22: prompt reuse and conversation navigation

The owner's request for more useful harness features adds Reuse on explicit user prompts, pane-local Find in conversation and Jump to latest. Reuse loads exact text without sending, confirms replacement of a different draft, rejects stale actions and retains current attachments without adding earlier ones. Find uses trimmed, case-insensitive literal source text, one result per matching prompt or reply, stable streamed reply keys and a current-message outline. It excludes thinking, tools and hidden goal instructions. Previous/Next, Enter/Shift+Enter and Cmd/Ctrl+F work in the active pane; Escape retains the result viewport and returns focus. Session swaps reset independent finders. Jump reuses the existing 120-pixel follow boundary: output preserves an older reading position until the reader jumps, then later output follows again. The full gate also exposed and repaired account busy-state publication at turn start and after Stop; account leases and routing remain unchanged. No HTTP route, fixture, dependency, provider configuration or account data changes. See [ADR 0036](../adr/0036-conversation-navigation-and-prompt-reuse.md).

Focused validation passes **60 Vitest tests in two files**, strict Svelte/TypeScript with zero errors or warnings, the UI build and **four Chromium scenarios**, comprising three new scenarios and the existing highlighting/copy regression. Units cover literal and inert queries, private-detail exclusions, streamed keys, exact prompt text, cancellation, newer drafts, selection, disposal and loading. Chromium proves no send during reuse, current attachment retention, focus, search counts, wraparound keyboard navigation, composition guards, no-match controls, hostile source remaining inert, narrow layout, independent split-pane state, session-swap reset and output before and after Jump. Six background screenshots were inspected in two bounded visual rounds; the manual design detector returned no findings. The compressed application bundle grows from 166.22 to 167.72 kB; Vite now emits its non-failing default 500 kB uncompressed chunk advisory. ## Slice 23: interactive terminal harness

The owner's request for a Claude-style CLI is delivered as `antiquota -i` with exact session resume. Bare account listing and arbitrary `run` argument forwarding remain unchanged; the named `chat` proposal is still absent. The harness provides editable multiline prompts, bracketed paste, history with draft restoration, unique slash completion, typed SSE replies, current model/mode/account context, explicit one-use tool approvals and Stop. Existing authenticated routes supply session/model/mode controls, cached account quotas, usage, git diff and compaction. Terminal and browser show one durable conversation. Prompt history stays in memory and rebuilds from that session's explicit prompts. See [CLI usage](../CLI.md) and [ADR 0037](../adr/0037-interactive-shared-session-cli.md).

Interactive startup discovers or starts a background engine, preserving it for other sessions after terminal exit. The new macOS PTY test initially exposed a real hangup failure: the subprocess had inherited the terminal's process group despite null standard streams. Unix startup now assigns a separate process group; Windows uses detached-process flags. The macOS regression proves the engine remains reachable after exit, keeps a no-account request without launching agy and then shuts it down through authenticated HTTP. Windows and Linux execution of this new interactive path is not claimed. [Red lifecycle test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-background-red.log), [green lifecycle regression](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-background-green.log).

Development attempts are recorded plainly: initial construction compile errors were corrected; the first PTY run passed 12 checks and failed one approval assertion because it matched an earlier input redraw. Corrected waits identify the command result and subsequent prompt. The first browser attempt failed on an ambiguous sidebar locator before CLI launch; it now targets the session button. Visual inspection caught a duplicated prompt echo; the follower now suppresses only the explicitly echoed prompt and has a replay regression. These are failed development runs, not hidden retries or final flaky passes. [Attempt record](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-initial-attempts.txt), [retained browser trace](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice23/initial-selector-failure/trace.zip).

The first full `just check` passed in **82.50 seconds** and `just test` in **523.01 seconds** before the later corrections: **504 Rust tests, 471 Vitest tests in 45 files and all 128 Chromium scenarios**, with zero failures, ignored tests, browser skips or flaky results in that run. The known abrupt-engine-exit case passes without a reset. All 476 frozen product/test hashes remain unchanged during the gates. No frontend production logic changed; existing captured HTTP replay, Vitest and browser regressions run in full. New Chromium proof runs the actual CLI in the existing xterm, observes the same prompt/reply in the browser and verifies shared engine identity after exit. Background screenshots were inspected at normal and narrow widths. Cursor movement uses Unicode scalars rather than compound emoji graphemes; this known limit is recorded in code and usage documentation. The existing non-failing Vite 500 kB raw chunk advisory and Node color-environment warnings remain visible. [Check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-check.log), [full test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-test.log), [results](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-final-results.json), [timings](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-gates.json), [source hashes](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-source-hashes.json), [visual review](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-visual-review.json).

Final source review then found an acknowledgement race: `approval_answer` returns the first decision, but the terminal originally printed its own requested answer. The CLI now displays the authoritative engine response and rejects a missing/unknown decision. The formatter unit passes. This issue was found by reading the connected flow, not by observing a live race. Full gates and packaging are repeated for the corrections; earlier successful gate/build logs remain under `slice23-before-ack-fix-*`. [Response regression](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-approval-ack-test.log).

The second full gate passed 505 Rust and 471 Vitest checks but failed the existing terminal resize browser scenario (127 browser passes, one failure). The preserved trace contains valid 42×76 and 42×51 shell reports, followed by separate reads containing only the prompt; parsing after a prior assertion produced NaN. Each measurement now has a unique nonce and parses the same polled snapshot. This repairs the test observation race without weakening its real shell-size and latest-resize comparisons. [Failed gate](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-before-resize-fix-test.log), [diagnosis](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-resize-diagnosis.json), [retained trace](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice23/resize-failure/trace.zip), [focused correction](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-resize-fixed.log).

The third full gate passed `just check` in 14.01 seconds but failed Rust replay after 142.68 seconds: the captured second account expected idle and reported busy. Fresh cached quotas ruled out a quota probe. Concurrent overview readers briefly acquire the same lease to inspect busy state; a new regression reproduced 113 false busy results in 512 idle reads. The adapter's existing shared mutex now serializes those readers. Captured values and worker leases are unchanged. [Failed gate](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-before-initial-state-fix-test.log), [red regression](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-overview-red.log), [fixed account suites and exact replays](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-overview-green.log).

A later check passed in 37.04 seconds, but its unfinished test gate was deliberately interrupted for a source-review correction: `/accounts` counted the two inactive zero-valued wall entries. It now counts only active cooldowns, with an explicit PTY assertion. The first focused command accidentally filtered out every test; the corrected fully qualified command ran and passed the intended contract. Neither the interrupted gate nor the empty focused run is claimed as proof. [Interrupted-run record](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-interrupted-review-gate.json), [retained test log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-before-wall-count-fix-test.log), [actual focused confirmation](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-wall-count-test.log).

Final corrected-source gates pass: `just check` in **19.72 seconds**, `just test` in **388.91 seconds**, with **506 Rust tests, 471 Vitest tests in 45 files and all 128 Chromium scenarios**. There are zero final failures, ignored tests, browser skips or flaky results. The known abrupt-engine-exit case passes without a reset. All 476 frozen product/test files remain unchanged through the gates. The fresh background screenshots were inspected at normal and narrow widths. [Final check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-check.log), [final test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-test.log), [results](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-final-results.json), [source review](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-source-review.json).

The current release build passes in **67.70 seconds**. The app bundle and read-only mounted DMG pass strict signature verification with hardened runtime and the audio-input entitlement. Bundled engine and UI hashes match this release; the mounted bundle matches the current app and contains the Applications shortcut. Release CLI help exposes interactive/resume and no chat command. The mount was detached. Packaging proof does not claim actual native permissions, window behavior or recording. [Build](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-release-build.log), [bundle verification](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-release-signature.json), [DMG verification](../../.handoff/evidence/slice23-dmg-proof.json).

F-1159–F-1162 bring the ledger to **903 Passed and 259 Pending out of 1162**. Only synthetic accounts and fake agy under Desktop/test are used: zero live tokens and zero provider time. The legacy checkout, provider configuration, live Chrome engine and existing native shell are not operated during this slice. Actual native window/tray/permission/AAC proof remains unverified here. Commits use [skip ci]; no Actions minutes are requested.

## Slice 24: terminal files, export and account controls

`antiquota -i` gains `/attach PATH`, `/attachments`, `/detach N|all`, `/export PATH`, `/accounts check|pause|resume SLOT` and `/routing [N]`. Each command uses an existing authenticated engine route; no route, state or file format changes. Staged files are sent in the existing `attachments` field, block session switches and survive a failed send with the draft. Exports use create-new with mode 0600 and never overwrite. See [CLI usage](../CLI.md) and [ADR 0038](../adr/0038-terminal-files-export-and-account-controls.md).

The first draft did not compile: the follower's user-event pattern missed the `steered` field. It now ignores the remaining fields. [Initial compile failure](../../.handoff/evidence/slice24-cli-initial.log).

[`terminal_files_exports_and_account_controls_use_the_engine_api`](../../tests/rewrite/interactive.rs) drives the real CLI in a PTY against an isolated engine and fake agy. It proves Unicode filenames, a missing-file error, the staged list, the switch guard, bounded detach, the saved user event with its attachment, an empty list after sending, private export with the full session, refusal to overwrite, pause and resume through HTTP state, invalid slot and action errors, routing bounds and routing output. A unit test bounds account actions and routing counts.

Gates on macOS: `just check` passes. Rust passes 509 tests and Vitest passes 471 tests in 45 files with zero failures. The first full test run failed every Chromium scenario at launch because Playwright's browser was not installed on this machine; after `playwright install chromium`, `just test-e2e` passes all 128 scenarios. An earlier check failed only because `pnpm` was not on the shell path. [Check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice24-check.log), [Rust and Vitest run](../../.handoff/evidence/slice24-test-no-browser.log), [browser run](../../.handoff/evidence/slice24-e2e.log).

No feature rows change; the ledger stays at **903 Passed and 259 Pending out of 1162**. Only synthetic accounts and fake agy under Desktop/test are used. Windows and Linux execution of the new commands is not claimed.

## Slice 25: compact activity, live usage and the native folder picker

The owner's requests of 2026-10-12 are delivered. agy's empty step replies no longer render, so adjacent tools fold into one activity row between the model's real text. Reads of `<name>/SKILL.md` show as "Load skill <name>" and lead the summary. Only the final reply keeps a visible Copy. Session token counters update after each completed step through an unstored `meta` event. The desktop app opens the system folder dialog for new sessions, and the dialog no longer chooses a model. See [ADR 0039](../adr/0039-compact-activity-live-usage-and-native-folder-picker.md).

Vitest proves empty replies are dropped before grouping and skill classification, including the refusal of piped commands. The app test `queued_turns_replay_and_delete_retains_usage` proves live counters build on earlier turns, are published and are never stored. Chromium proves the compact rows, the skill label, hover Copy, and the mocked native picker with cancel and choose. The layout test proves the dialog has no model control and still sends the saved default. [Screenshot](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice25/compact-activity.png).

The first full test run failed twice. The desktop `lib.rs` grew past the 500-line module limit, so the dialog commands moved to `dialogs.rs`. The captured `account-check-idle` replay saw a busy account once under full load; it passed five isolated reruns and the later full run, and is recorded as a flaky observation. [First run](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25-test-first.log).

Final gates on macOS: `just check` passes; `just test` passes 509 Rust tests, 473 Vitest tests and all 130 Chromium scenarios with zero failures. [Check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25-check.log), [test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25-test.log). Totals now include cached input everywhere: the ledger's day, account and model totals, session and turn "in" figures, and the CLI. `cached_input_reaches_the_ledger_and_the_usage_detail` proves the totals, and `sessions_saved_before_cached_input_rebuild_it_from_turn_ends` proves older sessions recover the count.

Metadata calls (quota probes, skill and hook lists, sign-in) now pass the default model, so agy no longer books them under its own default; the lifecycle test checks the probe argv. A goal left active when the engine stops comes back paused with Resume available; `an_engine_restart_pauses_working_goals_and_resume_continues_them` proves nothing runs by itself and Resume reactivates the goal. The existing terminal Stop test now polls for the saved stopped turn instead of reading once; under full load it had read the session just before the turn end was written.

The first final gate run passed check, 511 Rust and 474 Vitest checks but failed one Chromium scenario: the usage panel test still expected totals without cached input (375 instead of 1.3k). Its expected totals now include cached input, and it passes. The earlier contract that kept an interrupted goal active now expects it paused. [Run before the correction](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25b-test.log).

Transcript modes: compact (default), verbose and full. Vitest proves the compact projection keeps prompts, text, activity, errors and final goal outcomes while folding tools across hidden turns, and that stored modes map with `detailed` read as verbose. Chromium proves a goal with job check-ins reads as prompt, text, three activity rows and "Goal met" in compact, and that verbose and full show and open the expected rows and survive reload. The first gate failed svelte-check on a store used before its declaration and two browser tests that still drove the old checkbox; both were corrected. Final gates: `just check` passes, 511 Rust tests, 476 Vitest tests and all 131 Chromium scenarios pass. The browser suite passed again after the spacing change. [Check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25c-check.log), [test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25c-test.log), [compact screenshot](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice25/compact-goal.png).

The transcript toolbar moved into a "Conversation options" header menu. Chromium proves its items, the checked transcript view after reload, Find from the menu and from Ctrl+F with focus returning to the menu button, and copy and expansion through the menu. The first focused run failed two find tests because Ctrl+F still clicked the removed toolbar button; a per-session finder registry replaced it. Final gates: `just check` passes, 511 Rust tests, 476 Vitest tests and all 131 Chromium scenarios pass. [Check](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25d-check.log), [test](../../.handoff/evidence/slice25d-test.log), [menu screenshot](../../.handoff/evidence/ui-slice25/conversation-menu.png).

No feature rows change; the ledger stays at **903 Passed and 259 Pending out of 1162**. The real system dialogs on Windows and Linux are not exercised.

Logs and screenshots live in ignored `.handoff/evidence/`; local tokens and backup contents must never enter committed fixtures or this report.

The disabled-window scenario separately checks weekly reset parsing and its exact wall timestamp. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-042 | <a id="f-042"></a>[lifecycle::quota_cache_preserves_unknown_fields_and_disabled_window_uses_weekly_reset](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_lifecycle.rs). A quota envelope with a disabled 5h window lacking fraction and reset is returned as [0.0, null]. Its spent weekly window supplies the reset, producing the exact weekly reset plus 120-second wall while the other basket stays unwalled. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-043 | <a id="f-043"></a>[replay::captured_account_routes_preserve_exact_overviews_and_routing_records](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_replay.rs); [metadata_limits::huge_metadata_fails_promptly_preserves_cache_and_releases_lease](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_metadata_limits.rs). Captured quota probing returns parsed timestamps and windows without model turns. Oversized metadata returns promptly, preserves the previous cache and releases the lease. **Remaining:** No-model-token metadata, cache fields/windows and bounded output are covered. The exact 90-second timeout boundary remains unasserted. | Pending | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-044 | <a id="f-044"></a>[routing::tests::walls_need_two_percent_or_less_and_use_the_latest_applicable_reset](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [routing_edges::quota_panel_walls_at_two_percent_with_the_latest_spent_reset_or_the_fallback](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs). A window at exactly 2% walls the bucket and 2.01% does not. The latest reset among spent windows applies; a spent window without a reset uses the other windows' latest reset; with no usable reset the wall is now + 5 h 10 min. Each adds 120 seconds. Real HTTP probes set these walls and a later healthy reading clears them. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-045 | <a id="f-045"></a>[handoff::unreadable_quota_after_wall_uses_fallback_and_preserves_other_basket](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_handoff.rs). A genuine quota error followed by unreadable metadata persists the fallback wall. The test bounds its timestamp between the failed turn end plus 18600 seconds and the routing timestamp plus 18600 seconds, checks the reason, preserves the other basket and completes on the next account. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-046 | <a id="f-046"></a>[lifecycle::verification_probe_parks_and_then_restores_the_signed_in_account](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_lifecycle.rs). A verification probe first parks both baskets and stores its continue URL. A subsequent healthy panel clears both wall values to zero and removes the verification marker. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-047 | <a id="f-047"></a>[deterministic::routing_ledger_stamps_policy_time_and_auth_parks_exactly_thirty_days](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs); [lifecycle::auth_failure_parks_both_baskets_then_hands_off_without_token_changes](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_lifecycle.rs). With a fixed clock, a genuine auth failure parks both buckets until exactly now + 30 days with the signed-out reason, and a later healthy check clears both walls. The lifecycle test hands the turn off without changing token bytes. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-048 | <a id="f-048"></a>[routing_edges::model_buckets_keep_independent_walls_and_unwall_clears_both](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs); [routing::tests::paused_or_walled_accounts_never_rank](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs). A cg wall leaves the account available to Gemini sessions, an expired wall reads as no wall, and `antiquota unwall <account>` removes both seeded bucket walls of that account and no other. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-049 | <a id="f-049"></a>[tests::model_calls_share_an_account_and_probes_need_it_alone](../../crates/adapters/accounts/src/tests.rs); [concurrency::concurrent_sessions_use_different_account_homes_and_skip_busy_probe](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_concurrency.rs); [concurrency::python_account_lock_excludes_rust_probe_and_rust_lock_excludes_python](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_concurrency.rs); [placement::fill_first_runs_several_sessions_on_one_account_at_once](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_placement.rs). Manual checks refuse a busy account and the lease primitive supports shared calls and exclusive probes. Python and Rust locks exclude each other, including a shared Rust lease. With spread-first placement a concurrent session uses the other account's home. Automatic healthy and quota-failure probes now release shared model access and acquire the exclusive account lease. [post_turn_probes_defer_while_another_model_lease_is_held](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_placement.rs) proves both paths skip another shared call; [automatic_probes_exclude_new_shared_calls_until_the_reading_is_saved](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_placement.rs) proves their real lease blocks new calls through publication. The quota wall is published before release, and authentication/transient classification retains its guard. | Passed | [slice19-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice19-test.log) |
| F-050 | <a id="f-050"></a>[deterministic::warm_account_affinity_waits_ten_fake_seconds_before_another_lease](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs); [accounts_lifecycle.rs](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_lifecycle.rs). A controlled clock records exactly ten one-second waits for the preferred busy account before the second account runs the unchanged conversation. When the only eligible account stays leased, the waiting session remains cancellable and Stop returns it to idle without launching the runner. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-051 | <a id="f-051"></a>[lifecycle::stop_cancels_an_account_lease_wait_and_releases_admission](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_lifecycle.rs); [accounts_deterministic.rs](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs). Stop cancels a blocked lease-acquisition wait within two seconds, launches no runner and permits a later request after lease release. Repeated failures wall every candidate and finish idle with no queued request or extra account attempt. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-052 | <a id="f-052"></a>[deterministic::polling_uses_quota_poll_freshness_and_refreshes_only_stale_idle_accounts](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs); [cli_lifecycle::account_cli_probe_and_raw_quota_do_not_reconcile_active_engine_sessions](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_cli_lifecycle.rs). Refresh probes an account whose reading is exactly max_age old and skips one a second younger. It skips a leased account and returns the accounts that got a valid new reading, so an unreadable panel returns none. `refresh_accounts` now returns these names instead of nothing. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-053 | <a id="f-053"></a>[routing::tests::freshness_uses_minutes_with_a_two_minute_floor_and_a_ten_minute_fallback](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [deterministic::polling_uses_quota_poll_freshness_and_refreshes_only_stale_idle_accounts](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs). Freshness is quota_poll minutes in seconds with a 120-second floor. Non-numeric or non-finite text gives 600 seconds; surrounding spaces are ignored, as legacy `float()` did. The engine's poller waits 600, 120 and 300 seconds for `abc`, `1` and `5`. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-054 | <a id="f-054"></a>[routing::tests::headroom_is_the_smallest_fraction_and_the_latest_known_reset](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs). Headroom is the smallest window fraction and the expiry is the latest positive reset. A missing bucket or an empty window set stays unknown, and fractions without resets keep the expiry unknown. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-055 | <a id="f-055"></a>[routing::tests::perishable_spends_the_soonest_reset_then_more_headroom_with_unknown_expiry_last](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [rotation::perishable_uses_earliest_weekly_reset_and_round_robin_uses_last_pick](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_rotation.rs). Perishable ranking orders by soonest reset, then by more headroom at an equal reset. Accounts with unknown expiry come last, ordered by headroom with unknown quota counted as 50%. Input order does not change the result. The HTTP test picks the earlier weekly reset and keeps the warm account. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-056 | <a id="f-056"></a>[routing::tests::sticky_uses_sorted_names_and_round_robin_the_least_recent_pick](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [rotation::perishable_uses_earliest_weekly_reset_and_round_robin_uses_last_pick](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_rotation.rs); [routing_edges::paused_and_walled_accounts_sit_out_until_resumed_or_expired](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs). Sticky ranks by account name whatever the quota or history. Round-robin ranks by each account's last pick time, never-picked accounts first, and only pick entries count. Real HTTP turns follow both orders. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-057 | <a id="f-057"></a>[rotation::every_rotation_puts_an_account_below_reserve_after_healthy_accounts](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_rotation.rs). All three strategies put an account below the reserve after a healthy account without hard walling it. Pure ranking cases check fractions 0.029, 0.030 and 0.031 against a 0.03 reserve for sticky, perishable and round-robin, preserving preferred affinity at equality and above despite opposing strategy order. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-058 | <a id="f-058"></a>[routing_edges::paused_and_walled_accounts_sit_out_until_resumed_or_expired](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs); [routing::tests::paused_or_walled_accounts_never_rank](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs). With sticky rotation, a paused or currently walled first account is skipped for new turns, and it is chosen again after resume or once its wall has expired. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-059 | <a id="f-059"></a>[routing::tests::next_reset_is_the_earliest_live_wall_of_the_requested_bucket](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [routing_edges::all_walled_notice_reports_the_earliest_reset_of_the_requested_bucket](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs). The next reset is the earliest unexpired wall of the requested bucket, ignoring the other bucket and expired walls, or zero. When every account is walled, the wall notice carries that reset and no agy turn starts. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-060 | <a id="f-060"></a>[parser::tests::only_agy_error_channels_classify_a_failed_turn](../../crates/adapters/agy/src/parser.rs); [routing::tests::error_text_keeps_agy_error_channels_without_the_reply](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [ask::tests::answers_need_agy_json_and_pass_folder_access_without_skipping_permissions](../../crates/adapters/agy/src/ask.rs); [routing_edges::model_prose_and_successful_stderr_never_wall_an_account](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs); [cli_lifecycle::raw_current_success_ignores_historical_quota_error_without_walling_account](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_cli_lifecycle.rs). A reply full of error words and stderr warnings on a successful turn leave status SUCCESS, no wall and no routing decision. stderr counts only after a failed exit, and agy error frames count without their response field. The verifier and dictation path no longer classifies the reply text (W-026). | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-061 | <a id="f-061"></a>[routing::tests::classification_prefers_auth_then_quota_then_transient](../../crates/core/src/routing/tests.rs); [routing_edges::an_auth_error_outranks_quota_and_transient_words](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs). Each pattern of each class is classified; auth beats quota and transient, quota beats transient, and status codes must stand alone. Through HTTP, an agy error naming auth, quota and 503 together parks both accounts as signed out instead of walling them for quota. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-062 | <a id="f-062"></a>[handoff::quota_wall_hands_off_same_conversation_and_counts_current_steps_once](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_handoff.rs); [accounts_rotation.rs](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_rotation.rs). A quota-looking provider error triggers a real fixture quota probe. Exhausted allowance produces a quota-panel wall and handoff; remaining allowance of 0.9 produces a short cooldown with the rate-limit reason rather than an exhausted-quota wall. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-063 | <a id="f-063"></a>[rotation::rate_limit_with_quota_left_uses_escalating_short_cooldowns](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_rotation.rs). Zero, one and two matching recent cooldowns produce exact 60, 300 and 900-second records and strike reasons. Seeded old matching records, another slot, another bucket and a retry event do not increase those counts. | Passed | [slice9f-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice9f-test.log) |
| F-064 | <a id="f-064"></a>[deterministic::routing_ledger_stamps_policy_time_and_auth_parks_exactly_thirty_days](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs); [deterministic::quota_wall_ledger_entry_and_verification_parking_use_policy_time](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs); [replay::routing_reads_requested_tails_in_stored_order_and_skips_malformed_lines](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_replay.rs). With a fixed clock, the ledger holds the exact pick, retry, cooldown, signed-out, paused, resumed and wall entries with their policy times and fields. The replay test skips malformed lines. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-065 | <a id="f-065"></a>[tests::routing_history_trims_above_two_megabytes_to_the_newest_five_thousand_lines](../../crates/adapters/accounts/src/tests.rs); [routing_edges::routing_history_reads_default_to_sixty_and_trim_above_two_megabytes](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_routing_edges.rs). A history of exactly 2,000,000 bytes after an append stays whole; one byte more keeps the newest 5000 lines, ending with the new entry. A read without a count returns the newest 200. HTTP returns 60 by default and for an empty `n` (W-027). | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |
| F-066 | <a id="f-066"></a>[tests::today_starts_at_local_midnight](../../crates/adapters/accounts/src/tests.rs); [tests::local_day_usage_skips_malformed_rows_and_keeps_all_four_fields](../../crates/adapters/accounts/src/tests.rs). Malformed rows are skipped and all four totals are kept. | Passed | [slice13-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice13-test.log) |

| F-1011 | <a id="f-1011"></a>[architecture::core_does_no_input_output_and_its_tests_need_no_adapter](../../tests/rewrite/architecture.rs). Core has no workspace, Tokio, PTY, process, HTTP or clock dependency in its normal, dev or build dependencies, and its production source uses no filesystem, process, network, environment, thread or system-time API. Its own tests therefore run without any adapter. | Passed | [slice16-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice16-test.log) |
| F-1012 | <a id="f-1012"></a>[domain_tests::sessions_turns_goals_and_events_round_trip](../../crates/core/src/domain_tests.rs); [domain_tests::commands_round_trip_in_every_form](../../crates/core/src/domain_tests.rs); [domain_tests::accounts_slots_quotas_approvals_rules_and_settings_round_trip](../../crates/core/src/domain_tests.rs). `Session`, `Turn`, `Event` (in an `EventRecord` and a `StreamEvent`), every `Command` form, `Account`, `Slot`, `Quota`, `Goal`, `Approval`, `Rule` and `Settings` serialize and read back to the same JSON; unknown session and goal fields and large integers survive, and the default model stays `gemini-3.8-flash-high`. | Passed | [slice16-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice16-test.log) |

| F-1035 | <a id="f-1035"></a>[tests::queued_turns_replay_and_delete_retains_usage](../../crates/app/src/tests.rs); [sessions::restart_loads_valid_sessions_skips_broken_ones_and_fills_defaults](../../tests/rewrite/sessions.rs); [tests::unterminated_line_is_retained_without_corrupting_next_append](../../crates/adapters/store/src/tests.rs); [abrupt_engine_exit_after_init_resumes_the_durable_conversation](../../crates/server/tests/interruptions.rs). Each session appends its events to one log; a restart replays it to the same metadata and history in memory and on disk, a torn last line is kept without corrupting the next append, and an engine killed mid-turn resumes the same conversation. | Passed | [slice16-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice16-test.log) |
| F-1036 | <a id="f-1036"></a>[sessions::a_lagging_event_reader_is_disconnected_instead_of_skipping_events](../../tests/rewrite/sessions.rs); [stream.test.ts: uses one connection and requests a fresh snapshot on reconnect or invalid frames](../../ui/src/lib/stream.test.ts); [follow::tests::catch_up_shows_only_the_missed_saved_events_once](../../crates/cli/src/follow/tests.rs); [follow::tests::catch_up_keeps_waiting_while_the_goal_is_still_active](../../crates/cli/src/follow/tests.rs); [follow::tests::a_character_split_between_chunks_stays_whole](../../crates/cli/src/follow/tests.rs). The engine ends the stream of a reader that falls behind rather than skipping events. The UI reconnects and asks for a fresh snapshot. The CLI `goal` command now reopens the stream and shows the saved events it missed exactly once, keeps waiting while the goal is active, and decodes per frame so split characters stay whole. [queued_completion_is_confirmed_against_the_current_http_snapshot](../../crates/cli/src/goal.rs) additionally proves authoritative completion with equal or backward clocks; reply-prefix and queued-delta regressions prevent repeated output. | Passed | [slice19-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice19-test.log) |

| F-1148 | <a id="f-1148"></a>[tests::model_calls_share_an_account_and_probes_need_it_alone](../../crates/adapters/accounts/src/tests.rs); [placement::fill_first_runs_several_sessions_on_one_account_at_once](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_placement.rs); [concurrency::python_account_lock_excludes_rust_probe_and_rust_lock_excludes_python](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_concurrency.rs); [deterministic::warm_account_affinity_waits_ten_fake_seconds_before_another_lease](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_deterministic.rs). Two shared leases coexist while an exclusive one fails, and the account reads busy until the last is dropped. Three real sessions run at once on one account and home while a check of that account is refused. A shared lease also keeps the legacy exclusive lock out, and a probe-held account still gets the ten-second affinity wait. Automatic probes now acquire the exclusive lease too. [post_turn_probes_defer_while_another_model_lease_is_held](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_placement.rs) and [automatic_probes_exclude_new_shared_calls_until_the_reading_is_saved](../../tests/rewrite/accounts_placement.rs) exercise healthy and quota-failure paths with real shared locks and delayed probes. Verifier and raw failure callers consume their shared lease before probing. The full gate covers turns, verifier, raw runs, dictation and account maintenance. | Passed | [slice19-test.log](../../.handoff/evidence/slice19-test.log) |
